Here is the complete, detailed report on the EAGLE-3 directory and all related documentation. --- ## EAGLE-3 Full Pipeline Report ### Overview EAGLE-3 is a speculative decoding system being developed for the MiniCPM-SALA model. The goal is to replace the existing Medusa K=1 speculative decoding baseline (37.7% step-0 accuracy, 1.377 tokens/step) with a faster system targeting >55% accuracy and >2.5 tokens/step. The pipeline has 4 stages: data collection, training, weight conversion, and deployment. All 4 stages have working code, and training has completed 10 epochs. The current production server is still using Medusa, not EAGLE-3. --- ## 1. Data Collection (`eagle/collect_data.py`) **Purpose**: Send text prompts to the live NVFP4-quantized SGLang server and capture, for each EXTEND request, the intermediate hidden states from layers 1, 10, and 22, plus the top-256 logits. These become the training targets. **Trigger mechanism**: The collection is not a separate forward pass. It is a hook inside `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py`, activated by the env var `EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR`. When set to a directory path, the `_eagle3_collect()` function fires inside `MiniCPMForCausalLM.forward()` on every EXTEND forward, saving `.pt` files into that directory. **Activation in minicpm.py** (lines 91-161): - `_EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR` is read from env - `_EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS = [1, 10, 22]` (hardcoded) - `_eagle3_capture_layer(layer_idx, hidden_states)` is called inside the model's layer loop and caches into `_eagle3_aux_cache` - After the final layer, `_eagle3_collect()` concatenates the 3 cached hidden states into `aux_hidden` shape `(seq_len, 12288)`, computes top-256 logits from the final hidden states, and saves a `.pt` file **Note on what is captured**: At the start of iteration `i` in the layer loop, `hidden_states` holds the MLP delta output from layer `i-1` (before residual add). This is deliberately not the residual-added hidden state — it's the raw MLP delta. The collect_data script sends requests with `max_tokens=1` so the model does a single forward pass per prompt (no generation), and the hook fires once per request. **Data format saved per `.pt` file**: ``` token_ids: (seq_len,) int64 aux_hidden: (seq_len, 12288) bf16 -- cat(layer1, layer10, layer22) top_logit_values: (seq_len, 256) bf16 top_logit_indices: (seq_len, 256) int32 ``` **Training data budget**: - Total: 10,000 samples, each 2048 tokens - 50% SkyPile (Chinese, local jsonl at `/user_4813494d/data/skypile/data/2020-40_zh_head_0000.jsonl`) - 42% Code via `code_search_net` (Python 60%, Java 16%, Go 12%, JS 8%, PHP 2.4%, Ruby 1.6%) - 8% Wikitext-103 **Val OOD**: 64 samples from `bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` model responses, full length, no truncation. Requires server […]

Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile the full analysis. --- Here is a thorough analysis of every file, synthesizing all the details you asked about. --- ## 1. What does `mini_bench.sh` actually measure? `mini_bench.sh` is a fast (roughly 25% of the full-platform benchmark) local speed verification script. It operates by running `sglang.bench_serving` against a live SGLang server and measuring **Benchmark Duration (seconds)** — the wall-clock time to drain all requests — at three concurrency tiers. ### The Three Tiers | Tier | Dataset file | Full size | Default N (mini) | Max concurrency passed to bench_serving | |---|---|---|---|---| | S1 | `speed_bench_c1.jsonl` | 8 rows | 8 (uses all) | `--max-concurrency 1` | | S8 | `speed_bench_c8.jsonl` | 24 rows | 24 (uses all) | `--max-concurrency 8` | | Smax | `speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` | 64 rows | 64 (uses all) | (no flag — unlimited) | Note: the script header says defaults are N_S1=3, N_S8=8, N_SMAX=16, but the actual defaults in the script body are `N_S1=8`, `N_S8=24`, `N_SMAX=64`, which is the full dataset size. In practice, when called without arguments, mini_bench uses ALL rows in each dataset file. ### What the tiers are designed to stress - **S1** (1 concurrent request): tests single-stream latency / speculative decoding speedup. Prompt tokens range from 816 to 136,529 — the 8 items span short to very long context. - **S8** (8 concurrent requests): exactly one full batch at `--max-running-requests 8` capacity; designed to test batched throughput under mild concurrency pressure. - **Smax** (unlimited concurrency): all 64 requests fired simultaneously; exercises full scheduler saturation, memory pressure, and long-tail request handling. Prompt tokens range from 25 to 136,529. ### Dataset nesting / sampling logic The datasets are nested (S1 ⊆ S8 ⊆ Smax). When `N < full_size`, the script does **stratified sampling by prompt_tokens** (seed=42): it sorts rows by prompt length, divides into N equal strata, and picks one sample randomly per stratum. This preserves the long-tail distribution even in the mini run. ### What `bench_serving.sh` actually measures and outputs For each tier it runs `python3 -m sglang.bench_serving` and extracts the line: ``` Benchmark duration (s): <value> ``` The final JSON output is `{"S1": <float>, "S8": <float>, "Smax": <float>}` — three raw durations in seconds. **Lower is better.** There is no composite formula in the local bench; the platform scoring formula is in the eval pipeline (see below). --- ## 2. Operator Optimizations Active in the Submission ### A. MiniCPM Scale Triad (minicpm.py, lines 499-608, 651-732) Three per-layer scaling passes are applied — not computed on-the-fly but encoded as scalar multipliers baked into tensor operations: **Embedding scale** (`scale_emb`): `hidden_states = embed_tokens(input_ids) * config.scale_emb` (line 651 / line 717 for input_embeds). Applied once at model entry. **Residual scale** (`scale_depth / sqrt(num_hidden_layers)`): Every decoder layer uses `torch.add(residual, hidden_states, alpha=self.residual_scale)` (lines 601, 607). The `alpha` parameter makes this a single fused CUDA op — no separate multiply kernel. **Width scale** (`scale_width = hidden_size / dim_model_base`): Applied just before the lm_head logit computation: `hidden_states = hidden_states / self.scale_width` (line 732). This is the "scale absorption" — instead of having the LM head weight multiply by an extra constant, the hidden state is normalized here. These three work together as the MiniCPM scale-depth scheme (depth-dependent residual scaling + width normalization for numerical stability at large hidden size). ### B. Conditional RoPE and QK-Norm (lines 215-308, 331-438) **MiniCPMAttention**: RoPE applied only when `attn_use_rope=True` (config-driven). The `output_gate` (`use_output_gate`) is a sigmoid-gated attn output: `attn_output = attn_output * F.sigmoid(o_gate_output)` — a pure in-place gating pattern, no extra memory for intermediate activations beyond the gate projection. **MiniCPMLightningMixer** (GLA layers): QK normalization is conditional (`qk_norm=True` by default) — per-head RMSNorm applied on the Q/K head dim before RoPE. Scale mode is configurable: `1/sqrt(d)`, `1/d`, or `1.0`. Output norm (`use_output_norm`) and output gate (`use_output_gate` via `z_proj`) are also conditional. RoPE is applied on the flattened Q/K heads, then reshaped back for GLA input. ### C. GLA Kernel: Tuned Triton Decode Kernel (simple_gla_decode_kernel.py) This is the most significant kernel-level optimization. The FLA default kernel uses `BK=BV=min(next_power_of_2(K), 64)`, which for `head_dim=128` produces `128/64 = 2` blocks in K and V dimensions — 4x more blocks than needed. The custom kernel at `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/layers/attention/simple_gla_decode_kernel.py` forces `BK=BV=128`, matching MiniCPM-SALA's `head_dim=128` exactly, yielding a grid of `(NV=1, NK=1, N*H)` instead of `(4, 4, N*H)`. The header comment claims **~2.4x speedup at low batch sizes** (lines 1-9). The kernel fuses the recurrent update (`b_h = b_h * exp(b_g_gamma) + b_k * b_v^T`, then `b_o = sum(b_h * b_q)`) into a single Triton program. **GLA forward routing by seq_len (lines 1712-1735)**: - Decode (`seq_len=1`) and short sequences (`< 64`): `fused_recurrent_simple_gla` […]

I now have a complete and thorough picture of the entire system. Here is the full analysis. --- ## EAGLE-3 Integration with SGLang: Thorough Analysis --- ### 1. How EAGLE-3 Differs from EAGLE in the Worker Code **Algorithm detection** (`spec_info.py`, lines 17-44): `SpeculativeAlgorithm.EAGLE3` is a distinct enum variant. `is_eagle()` returns `True` for both EAGLE and EAGLE3 (EAGLE3 is a subtype for shared paths), while `is_eagle3()` returns `True` only for EAGLE3. **Token map (hot vocab) handling** (`eagle_worker.py` lines 128-140): EAGLE requires an external `speculative_token_map` file specifying the hot-vocabulary for draft head compression. EAGLE3 models embed their own hot-token vocabulary inside the model weights (`draft_model.hot_token_id`), so any externally specified map is ignored with a warning. The worker pulls `self.hot_token_id` from `self.draft_model_runner.model.hot_token_id` at line 178 instead. **Embedding sharing** (lines 165-189): EAGLE shares both embedding and lm_head with the target (always `set_embed_and_head(embed, head)`). EAGLE3 models typically do NOT share the lm_head — only `set_embed(embed)` is called, unless the draft model has `load_lm_head_from_target = True` (e.g. nvidia/gpt-oss-120b-Eagle3). **Auxiliary hidden state flag** (lines 198-206): EAGLE3 sets `self.eagle_use_aux_hidden_state = True` (default), then reads `eagle_config["use_aux_hidden_state"]` from the draft model's `hf_config` to override. If `use_aux_hidden_state` is False, EAGLE3 degrades to standard single-hidden-state behavior. The corresponding `eagle_aux_hidden_state_layer_ids` key from the config specifies which target layers to capture. **DP attention context** (lines 143-146): EAGLE3 with DP attention requires `draft_tp_context(get_attention_tp_group())` for the draft worker init, whereas plain EAGLE uses `empty_context()`. **Idle input sizing** (`forward_draft_extend_after_decode`, lines 974-979): When no tokens are verified, the idle hidden state placeholder is sized `hidden_size * 3` for EAGLE3 (3 concatenated aux layers), vs. `hidden_size * 1` for EAGLE. --- ### 2. The `aux_hidden_state` Flow **Target model — capture** (`minicpm.py`, `MiniCPMModel.forward`, lines 656-676): - `layers_to_capture` is a set populated by `set_eagle3_layers_to_capture()`. - The key design: the capture happens at iteration `i` (the TOP of the loop body, BEFORE calling `self.layers[i]`), so what is captured is the output of layer `i-1` (or the embedding at `i=0`). The method adds `+1` to each configured layer id so that `layers_to_capture = {val+1 for val in layer_ids}`. This aligns with the training data collector `_eagle3_capture_layer()`, which fires after each layer `i` completes. - `set_eagle3_layers_to_capture(None)` (fallback with no config) picks `{2, num_layers//2, num_layers-3}`. - The returned value is `(hidden_states, aux_hidden_states)` — a tuple when any captures happened. **Target model — set_eagle3_layers_to_capture** (`minicpm.py`, lines 767-775): Called from three places: `model_runner.py` at line 580 (after model load), `model_runner.py` at line 1748 (inside `run_forward_pass_for_cuda_graph_capture`), and `cuda_graph_runner.py` […]

Here is the comprehensive report: --- ## Medusa Speculative Decoding: Complete Analysis ### 1. Medusa Head Architecture **Class hierarchy:** `MedusaBlock` = `RMSNorm(4096)` + `ResBlock(4096,4096)` + `ResBlock(4096,4096)` Each `ResBlock` is: `x + SiLU(Linear(4096, 4096))(x)` (residual MLP with SiLU nonlinearity). Key details: - `Linear.weight` and `Linear.bias` are **zero-initialized** at training time. This means each head starts as a perfect identity transform, ensuring stable training and no initial output shift. - The `lm_head_weight` (73448 × 4096) is a **frozen buffer** (registered via `register_buffer`), NOT a `nn.Parameter`, so it contributes zero to the trainable parameter count. - The `MedusaBlock` class (with `RMSNorm` prefix) is the "newer" format; the simpler `ResBlock` format also exists for older checkpoints. Loading is auto-detected by scanning key names for `.norm.`, `.res1.`, `.res2.`. **Per-head parameter count:** | Component | Parameters | |---|---| | RMSNorm weight | 4,096 | | ResBlock 1 (Linear 4096×4096 + bias) | 16,781,312 | | ResBlock 2 (Linear 4096×4096 + bias) | 16,781,312 | | **Total per MedusaBlock (1 head)** | **33,566,720** | Deployed configuration: `NUM_HEADS=1`, `--speculative-num-steps 1`. **Trainable: 33,566,720 params = 33.6M = 67.1 MB BF16.** The frozen lm_head buffer (73448 × 4096 = 300.8M values, 602 MB BF16) is loaded from the target model and shared for logit projection. --- ### 2. Training Data **Budget v2: ~40M tokens (~9,765 .pt files of 4096 tokens each)** | Source | Tokens | Share | |---|---|---| | Chinese (SkyPile-150B): 3418 short + 366 long | 20.0M | 50% | | Code (code_search_net multi-lang): 3906 short | 16.0M | 40% | | English (wikitext-103): 781 short + 49 long | 4.0M | 10% | Code language breakdown (of the 16M code tokens): Python 50%, Java 20%, Go 15%, JavaScript 10%, PHP 3%, Ruby 2%. **Long sequences** (16K) pass through `_maybe_collect_hidden`'s chunking: each 16K request produces 4 separate 4096-token `.pt` files, so file count > sample count. **v3 code supplement** (`--code-supplement`): adds 5000 × 4096 = +20M code tokens, skipping the first 5000 samples per language to avoid overlap. Total after v3: ~60M tokens. **Validation:** - `val_ind`: 1.5% holdout of the training pool (random split, ≥10 files) - `val_ood`: `bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` — actual model responses from the benchmark, chunked to 4096-token windows. What is the OOD Accept Rate Metric? **Computed in `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/eval_ood_accept.py` (lines 246-283).** The process: 1. Apply the **shifted alignment** (lines 248-251): ```python shifted_ids = token_ids[1:] # x_1..x_{S-1} shifted_aux = aux_hidden[:-1] # aux_0..aux_{S-2} shifted_target_vals = top_logit_values[1:] shifted_target_inds = top_logit_indices[1:] ``` 3. Run draft model forward: `logits = model(shifted_ids, shifted_aux)` → shape `(S-1, 32000)` 4. Build target distribution by projecting the **top-256 target logits** into 32K draft vocab space (scatter with `amax`), then softmax → `target_p` of shape `(S-1, 32000)` 5. Compute position mask: only positions where `target_p.argmax()` (i.e., the target model's top-1 prediction in the 32K draft vocab space) corresponds to a token that IS in the draft vocab (`t2d[target_inds[:,0]].bool()`) 6. **Accept rate** = `(pred_draft_idx == target_draft_idx).sum() * mask / mask.sum()` Key metric properties: - It asks: does the draft model's greedy choice in 32K vocab match the target model's greedy choice in 32K vocab? - Only positions where the target model's top-1 is within the 32K draft vocab are counted - It is NOT "does the draft predict the actual next token" — it is "does the draft imitate the target model" **Measured counts:** 293,977 total positions (after shift), 285,248 valid (97.03%), 8,729 skipped (2.97%). --- ## 2. Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile the full analysis: --- ## Complete Analysis: Training EAGLE-3 Against the NVFP4 Model ### 1. Which model is used for data collection and training? Both `collect_data.py` and `train.py` point to the **same NVFP4 checkpoint**: ``` MODEL_PATH = [REDACTED] ``` - `collect_data.py` uses this path exclusively to load the **tokenizer** (`AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)`). - `train.py` uses this path to load **embed_tokens.weight** and **lm_head.weight** via safetensors (`load_embed_and_lm_head()`). - **Data collection is already running from the NVFP4 model.** The `collect_data.py` docstring explicitly says "Start NVFP4 server with: `EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR=/tmp/eagle3_collect`", and the collection hook `_eagle3_collect` is embedded directly in `/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py`, which runs inside the live quantized sglang server. --- ### 2. Does the NVFP4 model produce different hidden states / logits than BF16? **Yes, in two meaningful ways:** **a) Quantization noise in hidden states.** Every linear layer (q/k/v/o projections, gate/up/down MLP projections) runs through the NVFP4 W4A4 CUTLASS path or Marlin W4A16 path. The per-block scale structure (`weight_scale` shape `[N, K/16]` in FP8-E4M3, global `weight_scale_2` scalar, per-tensor `input_scale`) introduces quantization rounding error relative to BF16 at each layer. The error accumulates through 24+ transformer layers, so by layers 1, 10, and 22 (the `_EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS`) the hidden state tensors contain the NVFP4 quantization error distribution rather than the exact BF16 activations. The magnitude depends on GPTQ calibration quality (90 samples, up to 90K tokens per sample). **b) `lm_head` is NOT quantized.** `preprocess_model.py` phase 1 sets `model.lm_head = torch.nn.Identity()` before running GPTQ (to avoid quantizing it), and phase 2 explicitly restores the original BF16 `lm_head.weight` from the source model. So the **top-256 logits collected are computed with the original BF16 lm_head weight applied to the NVFP4-distorted final hidden state.** The logit distribution differs from pure BF16 only due to the accumulated activation error in `hidden_states`. --- ### 3. How the collection hook works end-to-end (is it already NVFP4-native?) **Yes, it is fully NVFP4-native.** The hook in `minicpm.py` operates as follows: ```python # Layer loop — inside the live quantized model _eagle3_capture_layer(i, hidden_states) # stores post-layer hidden_states for layers 1, 10, 22 # After all layers, in MiniCPMForCausalLM.forward(): _eagle3_collect(hidden_states, input_ids, forward_batch, _lm, self.scale_width) ``` Inside `_eagle3_collect`: ```python logits = torch.matmul(hidden_states / scale_width, lm_head.weight.T) # BF16 lm_head, NVFP4-produced hidden topk_vals, topk_ids = torch.topk(seg_logits.float(), 256, dim=-1) aux_hidden = torch.cat([cache[1], cache[10], cache[22]], dim=-1) # (seq_len, 12288), bfloat16 ``` `scale_width = hidden_size / dim_model_base = 4096 / 256 = 16` (matching `train.py`'s `SCALE_WIDTH = 16`). Let me compile the full findings: --- ## Medusa Weighted Sampling Infrastructure - Full Report ### Files Examined - `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/select_similar.py` (full file, 167 lines) - `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/train.py` (lines 40-55, 300-410, 138-193) - `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/data/train_ranked_by_val_ood.json` (first ~20 entries) --- ### 1. How `select_similar.py` Computes Importance Scores The pipeline has four stages: **Stage 1 - Feature extraction.** For each `.pt` file (both train and val_ood), `compute_mean_hidden` loads `data["hidden_states"]` (a 2D tensor of shape `(seq_len, 4096)`) and reduces it to a single 4096-dim vector by `h.float().mean(dim=0)`. Files with fewer than 64 tokens or any NaN values are discarded. Loading is parallelized with 16 threads. **Stage 2 - GPU cosine similarity matrix.** Both sets of mean vectors are L2-normalized via `F.normalize(..., dim=1)`, moved to GPU, then the full `(V, T)` similarity matrix is computed as a single matrix multiply: `sim = val_gpu @ train_gpu.T` where V=~150 val_ood files and T=~11,585 train files. **Stage 3 - Per-query top-K selection.** For each val_ood query, `sim.topk(K=50, dim=1)` retrieves the 50 nearest train files. The union of all selected indices forms a "subset" (tracked for reporting), but importantly this is not the final output. **Stage 4 - Continuous importance weight per train file.** Every train file (not just KNN-selected ones) gets an importance score: ```python max_sim_per_train = sim.max(dim=0).values.cpu() # (T,) ``` This is the **maximum cosine similarity** to any single val_ood query. The result JSON records `max_cos_sim` (the importance weight), `dist = 1.0 - max_cos_sim` (backwards-compat field), and `query_count` (how many val_ood queries named this file in their KNN top-50). The JSON is sorted descending by `max_cos_sim`, so the most "val_ood-relevant" training files appear first. --- ### 2. Similarity Metric **Cosine similarity of mean hidden states at the final layer (post-norm, 4096-dim).** The field in the `.pt` files is `"hidden_states"`, which from `medusa/collect_data.py` is the post-norm hidden state of the target model's last layer — the same tensor fed into `lm_head`. The mean-pooling over the sequence dimension reduces each document to a single embedding vector before computing cosine similarity. --- ### 3. How Sampling Weights Are Applied During Training In `train.py`, the weight computation (lines 382-400): ```python for f in train_files: sim = file_importance.get(str(f), 0.0) w = max(sim, WEIGHT_FLOOR) ** WEIGHT_POWER # WEIGHT_FLOOR=0.1, WEIGHT_POWER=2.0 raw_weights.append(w) ``` So `weight = clamp(max_cos_sim, 0.1) ** 2.0`. A file at cos_sim=0.93 gets weight ~0.865; a file at 0.0 gets floor weight 0.01. The power of 2.0 strongly […]

Now I have a complete picture. Let me compile everything into a thorough report. --- ## EAGLE Tree Verify and Batch Verify in SGLang: Complete Technical Report ### Overview EAGLE (Extrapolation Algorithm for Greater Language-model Efficiency) in SGLang uses **speculative decoding** with a token tree: a small draft model proposes multiple candidate continuations forming a tree topology, and the large target model verifies all candidates in a single forward pass. --- ## 1. How the Tree is Built from topk and num_steps ### Key Configuration Formula The number of draft tokens per sequence (`speculative_num_draft_tokens`, also called `num_verify_tokens`) is a user-defined hyperparameter. By default (auto-selected in `server_args.py:auto_choose_speculative_params`): - **LLaMA**: steps=5, topk=4, num_draft_tokens=8 - **DeepSeek/MoE**: steps=3, topk=1, num_draft_tokens=4 - **topk=1 special case**: `num_draft_tokens` is forced to `steps + 1` Note: `num_draft_tokens` does NOT equal `steps * topk + 1` automatically; it is separately configured and is typically much smaller than the full tree expansion to control memory cost. For topk=1, the tree is a linear chain of length `steps + 1`. ### Draft Forward Loop (`eagle_worker.py:draft_forward`, lines 618-688) ``` out_cache_loc shape: (batch_size, topk, spec_steps) # after reshape + permute ``` For each step `i` in `range(speculative_num_steps)`: 1. `select_top_k_tokens(i, topk_p, topk_index, hidden_states, scores, topk)` is called, producing `(input_ids, hidden_states, scores, tree_info)`. 2. `tree_info` = `(score_tensor, token_tensor, parent_index_tensor)` — these three lists are accumulated as `score_list`, `token_list`, `parents_list`. 3. For `i < num_steps - 1`: the draft model is forward-called with the new `input_ids` and `hidden_states`. Top-k logits/probabilities are extracted. **Step i=0 (first step) in `select_top_k_tokens`:** - `input_ids = topk_index.flatten()` — shape `(b*topk,)`: all topk tokens are proposed simultaneously. - `hidden_states` is repeated: `hidden_states.repeat_interleave(topk, dim=0)` — shape `(b*topk, hidden_size)`. Each request's hidden state is cloned topk times, once for each branch. - `scores = topk_p` — shape `(b, topk)`. - `tree_info[2]` (parent indices) = `arange(-1, topk)` repeated — the user_4813494d is -1, the first level has indices 0..topk-1. **Step i>0 (subsequent steps) in `select_top_k_tokens`:** - `expand_scores = scores.unsqueeze(2) * topk_p.reshape(-1, topk, topk)` — shape `(b, topk, topk)`. This multiplies cumulative path probability by new step probability. - `topk(expand_scores.flatten(1), topk, dim=-1)` selects the top-k best paths from `topk*topk` candidates — this is the **beam-search-like pruning** that keeps the tree manageable. - `input_ids` is gathered accordingly; `hidden_states` is selected to match the surviving paths. - Parent offsets: `topk_cs_index + (topk**2 * (i-1) + topk)` — encodes which node at the previous level is the parent of […]

With 32 attention heads, 2 KV heads (heavy GQA), head_dim=128: Q=32×128=4096, K=2×128=256, V=2×128=256 → total output 4608. Weight: 8192×4608 = 37.7M params. - Flash/paged attention kernel (single layer, 1 KV cache pool). - `post_attention_layernorm` + `mlp`: `LlamaMLP(4096, 16384, silu)` — gate_proj and up_proj each 4096×16384 = 67M params, down_proj 16384×4096 = 67M params. Total MLP: ~201M params, most expensive single component. - Final `norm` (RMSNorm 4096) and return. **Step D: lm_head** - `ParallelLMHead(32000, 4096)` — draft_vocab_size=32000. Weight: 4096×32000 = 131M params, ~262MB in BF16. - Note: This is separate from the target model's `lm_head` (which covers 73448 tokens). The hot_token_id mapping via `d2t` allows mapping draft IDs back to target IDs. **Approximate parameter count breakdown (BF16):** | Component | Params | VRAM (BF16) | |---|---|---| | fc (12288→4096) | 50.3M | ~100 MB | | qkv_proj (8192→4608, GQA 32/2) | 37.7M | ~75 MB | | o_proj (4096→4096) | 16.8M | ~33 MB | | gate+up_proj (4096→16384 each) | 134M | ~268 MB | | down_proj (16384→4096) | 67M | ~134 MB | | embed_tokens (73448×4096) | 301M | ~601 MB | | lm_head (32000×4096) | 131M | ~262 MB | | RMSNorms | ~0.05M | ~0.1 MB | | **Total** | **~737M** | **~1.47 GB** | Note: embed_tokens covers the full 73448-token vocab (shared model vocab), while lm_head only covers […]

好的，现在让我提供最终的报告： ## 调查总结：训练数据实际 token 分布 ### 关键发现 **按 token 计，代码实际占比：42.0%**（与按样本数的占比相同） 详细分布： - **Code**: 4,200 samples × 2048 tokens = **8.6M tokens (42.0%)** - **SkyPile**: 5,000 samples × 2048 tokens = **10.2M tokens (50.0%)** - **Wikitext**: 800 samples × 2048 tokens = **1.6M tokens (8.0%)** - **总计**: 10,000 samples × 2048 tokens = **20.48M tokens** ### 代码采样逻辑分析 代码中的采样方式非常统一： 1. **三个数据源都使用完全相同的截断策略** - 所有样本都被逐样本地编码成 token ID - 使用滑动窗口缓冲区，每次截取**恰好 2048 tokens** (`chunk = buf_ids[:SEQ_LEN]`) - 第 82 行：`SEQ_LEN = 2048` 硬编码 2. **代码没有特殊处理** - Code 采样（code_search_net）采用**相同的 2048-token 截断** - 不存在代码样本更长的情况（都在 2048 limit 内） - 代码按语言分配比例（Python 60%、Java 16%、Go 12% 等），但最终仍受 2048 token 限制 3. **采样方式特点** - 虽然代码函数平均字符长度更长（6000+ chars vs 3000+ chars），但这些都会被一视同仁地截断到 2048 tokens - 使用 `tokenizer.decode()` 确保 token 到文本的映射准确 ### 数据统计验证 从 `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/data/manifest.json` 验证： - manifest 中记录的 10,000 个样本与 code 中的 BUDGET 定义完全匹配 - 代码语言分布：Python 2498 (59.5% of code)、Java 672、Go 499、JavaScript 347 等 ### 结论 **您的假设不成立**。虽然代码样本的原始文本确实更长，但由于 SEQ_LEN=2048 的硬截断，token 分布与样本分布完全相同： | 数据源 | 按样本数 | 按 token 数 | 变化 | |--------|---------|-----------|------| | SkyPile | 50.0% | 50.0% | — | | Code | 42.0% | 42.0% | — | | Wikitext | 8.0% | 8.0% | — | 代码在设计上**确保了公平性** —— 不管原始文本多长，每个样本最后都贡献恰好 2048 tokens。

现在我有了完整的理解。让我生成最终的报告： ## 调查结论：NVFP4 原生训练对 EAGLE-3 Draft Head 的含义 基于代码库的详细调查，我理解了用户提到的"原生进行NVFP4训练 参考oss120b"在 EAGLE-3 draft head 训练上下文中的具体含义。 ### 当前状态（BF16 精度） **Eagle 训练流程（`/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/train.py`）**： - Draft model 以 **BF16** 精度训练（第62行：`DTYPE = torch.bfloat16`） - 模型架构：fc(12288→4096) + 完整 decoder layer + norm + lm_head（437M 参数） - 训练数据来自 **NVFP4 量化后的 target 模型**： ```python MODEL_PATH = [REDACTED] ``` - 数据格式：采集 target 模型的 layer 1, 10, 22 的 MLP delta 输出 + top-256 logits（来自已量化的 target） - **关键点**：draft 模型本身不做量化，始终 BF16，但学习的是来自 FP4 量化 target 模型的分布 ### "原生进行NVFP4训练"在 OSS 120B 上的参考含义 通过搜索代码库中关于 gpt-oss-120b 的引用： **在 SGLang 推理部分的注释**（`eagle_worker.py`）： ```python # but some models (e.g. nvidia/gpt-oss-120b-Eagle3) shares # [lm_head from target] ``` 这说明 **OSS 120B 是一个已有 EAGLE-3 draft model 的参考模型**，且该 draft model 在某些情况下与 target model 共享 lm_head。 ### 如果 Draft Model "原生进行NVFP4训练"需要的具体改动 如果要让 EAGLE-3 draft model 在 **NVFP4 精度下原生训练**（类似大模型的量化训练），技术上需要： #### 1. **架构层面改动** - 量化感知训练 (QAT) **在 `eagle/train.py` 中修改**： a) **导入量化库**： ```python from sglang.srt.layers.quantization.base_config import QuantizationConfig from llmcompressor.modifiers.quantization import GPTQModifier ``` b) **模型初始化时应用量化配置**： ```python quant_config = QuantizationConfig(scheme="NVFP4", ...) model = Eagle3Model(embed_weight, lm_head_weight, quant_config=quant_config) ``` c) **线性层替换为量化线性层**： ```python # 当前： self.fc = nn.Linear(AUX_DIM, HIDDEN_SIZE, bias=False) # 改为： from sglang.srt.layers.linear import QuantizableLinear self.fc = QuantizableLinear(AUX_DIM, HIDDEN_SIZE, quant_config=quant_config) ``` #### 2. **权重初始化策略** a) **权重初始化从目标模型的量化版本**： ```python # 当前加载 BF16 权重： with safe_open(...) as f: embed_weight = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float() lm_weight = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float() # FP4 原生训练应该： # 1. 加载 FP4 权重（解包） # 2. 初始化为伪量化权重（dequant → 模拟量化） # 3. 在训练中保持量化约束 ``` #### 3. **训练流程修改** a) **前向传播中应用伪量化**： ```python # 在 forward() 中每次前向时应用量化约束 hidden = self.fc(aux_shifted) # → 添加量化感知 # 类似于： if self.quant_config is not None: hidden = apply_fake_quantization(hidden, self.quant_config) ``` b) **梯度更新策略**： - 权重梯度计算后，在量化空间内进行更新（而非完整 FP32） - 确保权重始终在量化网格内 #### 4. **loss 和精度监控** ```python # 添加量化误差项到 loss（可选） quant_loss = compute_quantization_loss(model) total_loss = ce_loss + alpha * quant_loss # 在评估中监控： - 伪量化精度 (FP4 模型的 valid position accuracy) - 与完整精度 baseline 的 gap ``` #### 5. **具体参考实现** 代码库中已有类似框架的参考： - **Llama Eagle3 量化支持**（`/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/llama_eagle3.py`）： ```python class LlamaModel(nn.Module): def __init__(self, config, quant_config=None, ...): self.fc = torch.nn.Linear(...) # 需改为可量化层 self.midlayer = LlamaDecoderLayer(config, 0, quant_config, ...) ``` - **GPTQ + NVFP4 量化流程参考**（`/user_4813494d/openbmb/quant/gptq_nvfp4.py`）： - 使用 llmcompressor 的 GPTQModifier - scheme="NVFP4" 配置 - 可作为后量化的基础 ### 核心差异对比 | 方面 | 当前 (BF16) | NVFP4 原生训练 | |------|-----------|--------------| | **模型精度** | BF16 全精度 | FP4 伪量化 | | **权重形式** | 完整 FP32/BF16 | 量化网格约束 + FP8 scale | | **学习的分布** | BF16 target logits | NVFP4 量化 target logits | | **内存占用** | ~1.4 GB (safetensors) | ~0.35-0.4 GB (压缩 4 倍) | | **训练时间** | 基线 | 可能 +5-15% (量化开销) | | **推理速度** | 标准 BF16 (1×) […]

完美！现在让我总结全部发现： 根据我对SOAR竞赛评测工具和评分标准的全面调查，以下是详细报告： ## SOAR竞赛评测指标和验收标准完整调查报告 ### 一、核心评分指标体系 SOAR竞赛的评分包含**两个独立且并行的主要指标**： #### 1. 正确性评测（Correctness Evaluation） **文件位置**：`/user_4813494d/openbmb/toolkit/eval_model.py` **输出指标**： - `ori_accuracy`：模型在perf_public_set.jsonl数据集上的原始得分（百分比，0-100） - `overall_accuracy`：相对于基准分80分的归一化得分，计算公式为： ``` overall_accuracy = min(round(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100) ``` **评测数据集**： - 公开集：`perf_public_set.jsonl`（约50-100样本，包含不同长度的选择题MCQ和信息提取任务） - 私有集：`perf_private_set.jsonl`（内部保留，长度分布和任务类型与公开集一致，用于防止过拟合） **基准分设定**： - MiniCPM-SALA在该数据集上的原始得分约为82±2分 - 官方取**80分作为基准分**（从toolkit/README.md第103行） **任务类型及计分方式**： ```python - mcq（多选题）：精确匹配，提取ANSWER: X / \boxed{X}格式，得分0或1 - qa/niah/lcx（问答/长文本）：包含匹配，gold列表中任意一个候选答案即为完全正确，得分0或1 - cwe/fwe（实体提取）：覆盖率计分，需提取所有gold中的关键词，得分为hits/len(gold) ``` #### 2. 速度评测（Speed Evaluation） **文件位置**：`/user_4813494d/openbmb/toolkit/bench_serving.sh` **输出指标**： - `Benchmark Duration (s)`：完整测试耗时（秒） **测试结构**（3档并发度）： ``` S1: 并发度=1 S8: 并发度=8 Smax: 无并发上限 ``` **测试方式**： - 使用sglang官方`bench_serving`工具 - 通过**固定的模型输入和输出**来测试速度（不是真实生成） - 测试数据集格式： ```json {"question": "问题内容...", "model_response": "模型回答内容..."} ``` - 将输入/输出转换成conversations格式后，由bench_serving进行并发请求测试 **具体指标**： - Benchmark Duration统计的是**完整测试周期耗时**（秒） - 从输出中提取`Benchmark duration (s):`之后的数值 --- ### 二、关键发现：评分标准的真实情况 #### 用户质疑的澄清： **问题1：E2E提升是接受优化的唯一指标？** **答案**：**否**。官方同时关注两个指标： - `ori_accuracy / overall_accuracy`（正确性，权重未显式说明） - `Benchmark Duration`（速度） **问题2：ori_accuracy的门槛77.6/80是硬性的还是加权的？** **答案**： - 基准分是**80分**（不是77.6或80.6） - 这是**相对评估的参考基准**，不是硬性门槛 - 官方文档说"判断模型能力在修改过程中**是否会有所下降**"（toolkit/README.md 第103、117行） - 加权方式是：`overall_accuracy = min(ori_accuracy / 80 * 100, 2), 100)` - 若ori_accuracy=80，则overall_accuracy=100% - 若ori_accuracy=76，则overall_accuracy=95%（允许掉点） - 上限封顶在100%（即使ori_accuracy超过80也不额外加分） **问题3：速度测试的具体指标是什么？** **答案**： - **唯一输出的速度指标是：`Benchmark Duration (s)`**（总耗时，秒） - 这是"3档并发度下完整跑完所有评测请求的总时间" - bench_serving工具不直接输出吞吐量(tokens/s)或延迟(TTFT)，只输出总耗时 - mini_bench.sh中还会计算平均prompt_tokens（用于分层抽样），但不是最终得分指标 **问题4：有没有penalty机制（比如空响应扣分）？** **答案**： - **空响应问题已修复**（docs/empty-response-investigation-handover-20260413.md，2026-04-13） - 根因：旧版FlashInfer (0.5.3) 的CUTLASS FP4 GEMM在SM120上产生NaN - 修复：升级FlashInfer至0.6.7.post3 + cuDNN至9.20 - 平台验证结果：no-spec和spec_medusa_k1均零空响应，ori_accuracy=79.11% - **在eval_model.py中的计分逻辑**： ```python outputs[idx] = content if content is not None else "" # 空响应记为空字符串 # 后续score_mcq / score_exact_match 中，空字符串得分为0 ``` - 空响应会导致该样本得0分（无额外penalty） - 整体accuracy = (正确样本数) / (总样本数) - **没有发现其他penalty机制**（如答案过短、超时等会额外扣分） --- ### 三、最终评分整合方式 基于代码分析和文档审查，**官方未在公开文档中明确说明最终排名/得分的权重公式**。 **已知的评测流程**： 1. 正确性评测：得到`ori_accuracy`和`overall_accuracy` 2. 速度评测（3档）：得到S1/S8/Smax的`Benchmark Duration` 3. **综合方式**： - toolkit/README_EN.md第85行明确说"The final score is subject to the official evaluation environment" - eval/run_public_eval_full.py和toolkit/eval_model.py都输出两个独立的指标对象，未见明确的权重组合 - AGENTS.md第一条建议："启服务后跑公开集 accuracy，必要时再补 speed bench"，暗示accuracy是首要指标 **推理的评分权重模式**： - 竞赛官方很可能采用**两步式评分**： 1. **门槛验证**：ori_accuracy≥某阈值（可能75-80分之间）合格 2. **速度排名**：在合格的提交中，按Benchmark Duration从低到高排名 - 但未在公开文档中明确此阈值 --- ### 四、具体评测指标汇总表 | 指标 | 源文件 | 计算方式 | 输出格式 | 权重/用途 | |------|--------|---------|---------|----------| | ori_accuracy | eval_model.py:L432 | (正确样本数/总样本数)×100 | 浮点百分比(0-100) | 正确性评估，与80分基准比较 | | overall_accuracy | eval_model.py:L433 | min(ori_accuracy/80×100, 100) | 浮点百分比(0-100) | 相对基准得分，上限100% | | Benchmark Duration (S1) | bench_serving.sh:L162 | 并发度=1时的总耗时 | 浮点秒数 | 低并发场景速度 | | Benchmark Duration (S8) | bench_serving.sh:L162 | 并发度=8时的总耗时 | 浮点秒数 | 中等并发场景速度 | | Benchmark Duration (Smax) | bench_serving.sh:L162 | 无并发限制时的总耗时 | 浮点秒数 | 高并发场景速度 | --- ### 五、验收标准判断 **模型能力下降判断**（toolkit/README.md第103-117行）： - 通过ori_accuracy与80分基准的相对值来判断 - 如ori_accuracy从原始82分降至70分，则overall_accuracy=87.5%，被判定为有掉点 **评测合格性**： - 没有看到明确的"必须≥77.6分"的硬性要求 - 官方通过公开集和私有集的分布一致性来防止过优化 --- ### 六、结论 **用户的质疑"E2E提升是接受优化的唯一指标"是错误的**。SOAR竞赛官方明确区分了： 1. **正确性指标**（ori_accuracy / overall_accuracy）——必须保证模型能力不显著下降 2. **速度指标**（Benchmark Duration S1/S8/Smax）——优化的目标 这两个指标都被官方评测系统记录和输出，但最终排名权重公式在公开文档中**未明确揭示**。基于评测工具的优先级和AGENTS.md的建议，合理推断是：**先验证accuracy合格性，再按speed排名**。

完美！现在让我生成最终的总结报告： ## 最终调查报告：OOD 验证数据 (val_ood) 的 Token 分布 ### 数据源和规模 - **JSONL 源数据**: `/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` 包含 **64 条记录** - **生成的 .pt 文件**: `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/data/val_ood/` 中有 **191 个文件**（经过 4096-token 分块） - **实际 Token 总量**: **416,278 tokens** ### 核心发现：按 Token 计的实际分布 | 类别 | 记录数 | % 记录 | Token 数 | % Token | 平均 Token/记录 | |------|--------|--------|---------|---------|-----------------| | **编程能力 (Programming)** | 12 | 18.8% | **244,799** | **58.8%** | 20,400 | | **长文本 (DeepResearch)** | 49 | 76.6% | **118,735** | **28.5%** | 2,423 | | **数学能力 (Math)** | 2 | 3.1% | **34,684** | **8.3%** | 17,342 | | **文本生成 (Text Generation)** | 1 | 1.6% | **18,058** | **4.3%** | 18,058 | | **合计** | **64** | **100%** | **416,278** | **100%** | — | ### 验证用户观察："代码条数少但 token 数占绝大多数" **✓ 确认属实** - 编程类仅有 **12 条记录**（占 18.8%），但生成 **244,799 tokens**（占 **58.8%**） - 平均每条编程记录产生 **~20,400 tokens** - 相比之下，长文本虽有 49 条记录（占 76.6%），但仅产生 118,735 tokens（占 28.5%） - **平均每条长文本记录只产生 ~2,423 tokens** **Token 倍数关系**: 编程类产生的 token 数是长文本的 **2.1 倍**，尽管记录数只有 1/4 ### 按 Token 计的真实占比 用户原来说：49 deepresearch（58%), 12 programming（19%), 2 math, 1 text-gen **实际按 Token 计**： - **编程**: 58.8% ← 从 19% 上升到 58.8%（增加 3.1 倍） - **DeepResearch**: 28.5% ← 从 58% 下降到 28.5%（下降 2.1 倍） - **数学**: 8.3% - **文本生成**: 4.3% ### 技术细节 - 有效的字符-to-token 比率：**1.52 字符/token**（这反映了混合语言和代码的 tokenizer 效率） - 编程类响应平均字符数：**30,971** (最小 4,846，最大 51,168) - 长文本响应平均字符数：**3,679** (最小 11，最大 30,058) - **差异原因**：代码生成任务产生长的、完整的代码片段和解释，而长文本类的很多"记录"其实只是从长文档中提取的短摘要或查询结果 ### 文件位置参考 - 源 JSONL: `/user_4813494d/openbmb/bench/data/speed_bench_cunlimited.jsonl` - 生成的 .pt 文件: `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/data/val_ood/`（001487.pt - 013677.pt） - 元数据: `/user_4813494d/openbmb/medusa/data/meta.json`

Let me compile my findings. ## RESEARCH FINDINGS ### **Topic 1: Native NVFP4 Training** **NVFP4 Format:** NVFP4 (float4_e2m1f) is a 4-bit floating-point format with 1 sign bit, 2 exponent bits, and 1 mantissa bit. It uses two-level scaling: E4M3 (FP8) per 16-value micro-block plus FP32 per-tensor global scale. This achieves 3.5x memory reduction vs FP16 with <1% accuracy loss on key benchmarks. **Three Distinct Approaches:** 1. **PTQ (Post-Training Quantization):** Weights quantized after training without retraining; simplest, lowest cost, but accuracy loss. 2. **QAT (Quantization-Aware Training / Fake Quantization):** Simulate quantization during fine-tuning using dequantize-recompute operators; weights remain in full precision. This is what ModelOpt and NVIDIA's current QAT workflows implement. 3. **Native FP4 Training:** All forward/backward computations performed directly in FP4 (no fake quantization); requires differentiable quantization estimator and outlier clamping. This is what the recent Quartet and "FP4 All the Way" papers demonstrate. **NVIDIA's Official Support:** - ModelOpt supports QAT workflows (fake quantization) for FP4, not true native training yet - NVFP4 native training is enabled by Blackwell's Tensor Cores (first GPU to natively support FP4) - NVIDIA has officially validated FP4 training at scale: first company to achieve MLPerf Training v5.1 accuracy with FP4 calculations - No current evidence of "FP4-initialized training" as a distinct NVIDIA concept; literature focuses on QAT vs full native training **Research Foundation:** - **Quartet** (arXiv:2505.14669): Shows native FP4 training can match BF16 baselines with proper initialization (cosine similarity, projection magnitude alignment) and unbiased gradient estimation - **Quartet II**: Improves NVFP4-specific training with better gradient estimation - **NVFP4 Training Blog**: NVIDIA highlights 2x speedup vs FP8 on Blackwell for linear layers; native training achieves FP16-like accuracy ### **Topic 2: Hybrid Marlin+CUTLASS for Draft Model Inference** **From Local Codebase Analysis:** The target model uses hybrid dispatch at threshold `_MARLIN_HYBRID_THRESHOLD` (env var `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD`, default 0 = disabled): - M ≤ threshold → Marlin FP4 W4A16 (quantized weights, FP16 activations) - M > threshold → CUTLASS NVFP4 W4A4 (quantized weights & activations) **For EAGLE-3 Draft Model (M=1-6):** 1. **M Always ≤ 6** (single token decode at M=1, tree verify at M≤6): Marlin FP4 W4A16 is **universally optimal**. No practical case for CUTLASS W4A4. 2. **Marlin W4A16 Advantages at Small M:** - Specialized GEMV kernel (general matrix-vector for M=1) - Avoids activation quantization overhead - Lower launch overhead vs CUTLASS - Optimized memory reuse for decode workloads 3. **Small N Layers (k_proj […]

Here is the research report. --- ## FP4 (float4_e2m1f) 量化方法对比研究 — EAGLE-3 Draft Model ### FP4 格式基础 FP4 E2M1 有 16 个可表示值（含符号位）：`{0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0}` 正负各 8 个。间距**非均匀**：低值区间隔 0.5，高值区间隔 2.0。NVFP4 格式使用两级缩放：per-block FP8 scale（每 16 个权重一组）+ per-tensor FP32 global scale。Marlin W4A16 推理保持 BF16 激活，只量化权重。 ### 各方法分析 **1. RTN（Round to Nearest）** 直接将 BF16 权重除以 block scale，round 到最近的 FP4 值。代码库中 `bench_nvfp4_gemv.py` 已有完整实现（第 78-97 行）。对大模型效果尚可，但 437M 小模型缺乏冗余，RTN 通常导致显著精度退化。优势：零开销、可嵌入训练循环。 **2. GPTQ（二阶优化）** 代码库已有完整实现用于目标模型（`preprocess_model.py`、`gptq_quantize_fouroversix.py`）。核心机制：利用 Hessian 逆矩阵逐列量化权重，将量化误差通过 `err * H_inv` 传播补偿到后续列。GPTQ 对量化网格无要求——`fake_quantize` 已支持 FP4 E2M1 的非均匀映射（通过 `FP4_E2M1_DATA.cast_to_fp4`），且 FourOverSix 已集成（per-block 自适应选择 scale=4 或 scale=6）。**但 GPTQ 是后训练方法**，需要预训练好的 BF16 权重 + 校准数据。对从零训练的 draft model 不适用。 **3. AWQ（Activation-Aware Weight Quantization）** 代码库中 `awqlite_nvfp4.py` 使用 `mtq.NVFP4_AWQ_LITE_CFG` 调用 NVIDIA ModelOpt 进行 AWQ-lite NVFP4 量化。AWQ 通过激活重要性缩放保护关键通道。同样是后训练方法，不适用于从零训练场景。 **4. W-QAT（权重投影 QAT）** 每个 optimizer step 后，将权重投影到 FP4 网格（`w_q = fp4_round(w / scale) * scale`），推理用量化权重，但 optimizer 维护全精度 master copy。梯度通过 STE 传递。这**适合从零训练**——在整个训练过程中权重始终被约束在 FP4 可表示范围内。实现简单：在 `eagle/train.py` 的 optimizer.step() 后加一个投影即可。关键问题：FP4 只有 8 个正值，每 16 个权重一个 scale，优化可能不稳定。 **5. STE-QAT（Forward Fake Quantization）** Forward pass 中插入 fake quantize（`q = round_to_fp4(w)`），backward 中用 STE（`grad_w = grad_q`）。与 W-QAT 的区别：不修改 optimizer 中的权重，只在 forward 模拟量化效果。`compressed_tensors.quantization.fake_quantize` 已支持 FP4 E2M1 格式，代码可直接复用。**适合从零训练**，且与 GPTQ 的 fake_quantize 路径一致。 **6. NVIDIA ModelOpt QAT** ModelOpt 提供 `mtq.quantize` + fine-tuning 流程，主要面向大模型 PTQ→QAT 精调场景。代码库中 `loader.py:2477` 有集成点。ModelOpt 的 FP4 QAT 使用其内部 STE 实现，但核心原理与方法 5 相同。对 437M 小模型从零训练，直接使用 fake_quantize + STE 更灵活，无需引入 ModelOpt 训练依赖。 ### 关键发现 1. **GPTQ 完全支持 FP4 非均匀网格**——代码库已验证（`FP4_E2M1_DATA.cast_to_fp4` 处理映射），但仅限后训练。 2. **从零训练场景只有 QAT 可行**（方法 4 或 5）。推荐 STE-QAT：在 `eagle/train.py` 的每个 nn.Linear 的 forward 中插入 `fake_quantize`，用现有 `compressed_tensors` 库，无需额外依赖。 3. **Draft model 量化的文献空白**：搜索未发现专门针对 speculative decoding draft model 量化的论文。EAGLE/EAGLE-2/EAGLE-3 论文均使用 BF16 draft model。Medusa 论文同样未讨论 head 量化。这是一个实践导向的工程问题。 4. **小模型 FP4 的预期退化**：FP4 只有 8 个有效精度级别（3-bit 有效精度）。437M 模型的冗余远低于 7B+，RTN 可能导致 accept rate 下降 30-50%。STE-QAT 让模型在训练中适应量化约束，预期退化 5-15%。 5. **Marlin W4A16 格式要求**：需要 NVFP4 packed 格式（`(N, K/2)` uint8 + `(N, K/16)` FP8 block scales + FP32 global scale），通过 `gptq_marlin_repack` 转换为 Marlin tile 格式。QAT 训练完成后需执行一次最终量化 + repack。 ### 推荐方案 **STE-QAT 从零训练**：在 `eagle/train.py` 中对每个 Linear 层的 forward 插入 FP4 fake quantize（复用 `compressed_tensors.quantization.fake_quantize`），训练完成后执行一次 RTN 最终量化 + Marlin repack。这是唯一无需预训练 BF16 checkpoint 的高质量方案。

Here is my analysis: --- **根因分析：eval_ood 76.3% vs SGLang accept_rate 7%** 核心差异在于 **eval_ood 的指标定义被严重膨胀**，与 SGLang 在线 verify 的判定标准不同： **差异 1: target_mask 过滤（最关键）** - `eval_ood` 用 `target_mask = t2d[target_argmax_full]` 过滤掉 target top-1 不在 draft_vocab (32000) 中的位置，**只在 target top-1 ∈ draft_vocab 的位置计算正确率** - SGLang 在线 verify 没有这个过滤。`target_predict = argmax(target_logits)` 对全部 73448 vocab 做 argmax，如果 target 选了 draft_vocab 外的 token（id ≥ 32000），draft 永远无法预测对，但 eval_ood 会跳过这些位置 - 假设只有 ~60% 的位置 target top-1 落在 draft_vocab 内，eval_ood 的有效分母被缩小了约 40% **差异 2: 比较对象不同** - `eval_ood`: `pred_idx == target_draft_idx`，其中 `target_draft_idx = _build_target_p(top256_logits).argmax`，是在 draft_vocab 空间内对 top-256 logits 做 softmax 后的 argmax。这是一个 **近似的** target 分布 - SGLang: `target_predict = argmax(full_vocab_logits)`，直接对完整 73448 维 logits 取 argmax，然后与 draft token（已通过 `hot_token_id` 映射到 full vocab）比较 **差异 3: 量化差异** - `eval_ood` 使用 FP4_QAT 的 fake-quantize（训练时模拟），fc 层是 `FP4QATLinear` - SGLang 使用 NVFP4 CUTLASS 或 Marlin W4A16 实际 dequantize，存在舍入差异 **结论**：根因是 eval_ood 用 `target_mask` 跳过了 draft_vocab 覆盖不到的位置，并用 top-256 近似 target 分布做比较，严重高估了实际 accept rate。SGLang 对全 vocab argmax 比较，无过滤。建议在 eval_ood 中去掉 `target_mask` 并改用 `d2t[target_argmax_full]` 映射后与 draft argmax 比较，得到的数字才与在线 accept rate 可比。

Here is the complete report: --- ## EAGLE-3 训练代码详细对比报告 ### 文件对照 | 组件 | 原版 | 我们的 | |---|---|---| | 模型定义 | `/user_4813494d/EAGLE/eagle/traineagle3/cnets.py` | `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/train.py` L126-488 | | 训练循环 | `/user_4813494d/EAGLE/eagle/traineagle3/main.py` | `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/train.py` L672-end | | Config | `/user_4813494d/EAGLE/eagle/traineagle3/config.json` + `configs.py` | 硬编码常量 (L40-66) | --- ### 差异 1: Attention 缺少 RoPE [CRITICAL] **原版** (`cnets.py` L205-256): ```python self._init_rope() # 初始化 LlamaRotaryEmbedding # forward: cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(query_states, seq_len=q_len + lck) query_states, key_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(query_states, key_states, cos, sin, position_ids + lck) ``` 原版对 Q 和 K 都应用了 RoPE，且 position_ids 会加上 `lck`（当前 cache 里已有的 KV step 数量），使得每个 TTT step 的 position 偏移递增。 **我们的** (`train.py` L139-226): Eagle3Attention 完全没有 RoPE。没有 `rotary_emb`、没有 `apply_rotary_pos_emb`、没有 `position_ids` 参数。Step 0 使用 `F.scaled_dot_product_attention(is_causal=True)` 代替手动 attention 计算。 **影响**: 极其严重。RoPE 为 attention 提供位置信息。缺少 RoPE 意味着 attention 是位置无关的（仅靠 causal mask 提供顺序约束）。这会导致： 1. 模型无法学习真正的位置依赖关系 2. 训练出的权重与有 RoPE 的推理环境不匹配 3. 多步 TTT 的 position 偏移语义完全丢失 --- ### 差异 2: TTT 步数 7 vs 1 [CRITICAL] **原版** (`cnets.py` L498): `self.length = 7`，训练 7 个 TTT 步。每步 shift input_ids 和 target 一次（`padding(x, left=False)` = 左移 1 位，末尾补零），KV cache 在步间累积。loss 用 `0.8^i` 加权。 **我们的** (`train.py` L52): `TTT_STEPS = 1`。只训练 1 步，没有多步预测。 **影响**: 极其严重。EAGLE-3 的核心设计是通过多步 TTT（test-time training）让 draft model 在推理时逐步自适应。只训练 1 步意味着： 1. KV cache 累积机制从未被训练（cache 相关代码形同虚设） 2. 模型只会做 1-step 预测，无法在推理时通过多步 TTT 提升质量 3. loss 加权衰减无意义（只有一个 loss 项） --- ### 差异 3: Aux Layer 选择不同 [IMPORTANT] **原版** (`modeling_llama_kv.py` L1139): ```python if idx==len(self.layers)-3 or idx==len(self.layers)//2 or idx==2: all_hidden_states += (hidden_states,) ``` 对 Llama 3 8B (32层)，选的是 layer 2, 16, 29（输入到这些层之前的 hidden state）。 **我们的** (`train.py` L9, CLAUDE.md): 使用 layer [1, 10, 22] 的输出。 **影响**: 重要。不同的 aux layer 提供不同质量的中间表征。原版选择的是 early(2), middle(16), late(29) 层。我们选择的 [1, 10, 22] 偏前，可能包含的高层语义信息不足。不过这一差异可能是因为 MiniCPM-SALA 架构不同于 Llama，需要独立验证哪组 aux layers 最优。 --- ### 差异 4: 在线 dataprepare vs 离线数据采集 [IMPORTANT] **原版** (`cnets.py` L713-731): 训练时在线运行 target model 获取 hidden states 和 logits。`dataprepare()` 每个 batch 都跑一遍完整的 target model forward： ```python outs = self.target_model(input_ids=input_ids, attention_mask=attention_mask) hidden_states0 = outs.hidden_states[0] # layer 2 input hidden_states1 = outs.hidden_states[1] # layer 16 input hidden_states2 = outs.hidden_states[2] # layer 29 input target = outs.logits # 完整 vocab logits ``` Target logits 是**完整 vocab** (128256 维)，softmax 后截取 draft vocab。 **我们的** (`train.py` L492-513): 使用离线预采集的 `.pt` 文件，包含 `{token_ids, aux_hidden, top_logit_values, top_logit_indices}`。Target logits 只保存了 **top-256**，然后用 `_build_target_p()` scatter 到 draft vocab 空间重建分布。 **影响**: 重要。 1. 原版用全 vocab logits 做 softmax，得到的 target 分布精确。我们只用 top-256 logits 近似，低概率 token 的概率被强制设为接近 0（通过 -1e9 填充），softmax 分布与原版不完全一致。 2. 原版的 `target_head = target_head[..., self.t2d]` 是直接对完整 logits 按 bool […]

完美，现在我有全面的信息了。让我生成报告： ## 分析报告 **SGLang Decode 循环的关键同步开销：** ### 1. **GPU→CPU 同步点（3处）** - `process_batch_result_decode`（行363）：`result.copy_done.synchronize()` 阻塞等待上一批的H2D传输完成 - `copy_to_cpu()`（utils.py行51-77）：`next_token_ids.to("cpu")` 异步，但`copy_done.record()`建立同步屏障 - `process_batch_result_prefill`（行94）：相同同步机制 ### 2. **后处理中的 .tolist() 开销** - `process_batch_result_decode`（行372）：`next_token_ids.tolist()` 隐含CPU同步 - `process_batch_result_prefill`（行109）：同样的张量序列化同步 - logprobs转移：行374 `next_token_logprobs.tolist()` ### 3. **调度循环框架开销**（不可跳过） - 每decode step 必须经历：`get_next_batch_to_run()`→`run_batch()`→`process_batch_result()`→`get_model_worker_batch()` - `get_model_worker_batch()`（schedule_batch.py行2321）构建100+字段的ModelWorkerBatch对象，**无持续batch优化** - 无`num_continuous_decode_steps`实现，每step都重复调度逻辑 ### 4. **可优化的关键点** - 采样后处理（行372-374）：.tolist()前的同步可延迟到流输出时 - 前向流与默认流的显式同步（行2240、2343）可精细化 - TP全约约束（行2414-2417）：`tensor.tolist()`强制阻塞 **200字总结：** Decode循环主要开销来自三处GPU→CPU同步（copy_done、.tolist()、logprobs转移）和不可跳过的调度框架。每step都重建ModelWorkerBatch（100+字段拷贝）且无批量decode优化机制。建议：(1)批量化.tolist()操作到输出阶段;(2)实现num_continuous_decode_steps跳过调度循环;(3)缓存ModelWorkerBatch构建。

现在我有了足够的信息。让我撰写最终报告： ## 分析总结：MiniCPM-SALA decode 时序瓶颈 经过深入分析SGLang中的decode执行流程，已发现除已知优化外的**三个可行加速点**： ### 1. **采样中的GPU-CPU同步（关键）** 在 `scheduler_output_processor_mixin.py:372-374` 处： ```python next_token_ids = next_token_ids.tolist() # GPU→CPU阻塞同步 next_token_logprobs = logits_output.next_token_logprobs.tolist() ``` 每次decode循环强制阻塞同步，等待采样结果回传CPU。该同步是**必需的**（用于检查finish条件和更新状态），但可以与下一步forward重叠：目前是串行执行，无重叠。 ### 2. **缺失多步连续decode** 代码中 `num_continuous_decode_steps` 参数（server_args.py:569）**存在但未使用**。当前采样后需检查finish和更新request状态，导致无法连续运行多步。可考虑： - 延迟finish检查（采样后先入队下一forward，检查异步进行） - 或在decode前批量预检查 ### 3. **logits_processor中的TP同步** `logits_processor.py` 中对多TP rank的logits处理涉及all_gather操作，逐token执行。decode时批量单token预测，每步都是同步点。可合并多步的all_gather为单次collective。 ### **最快可实现方案** 采样结果的tolist()与下一batch forward重叠（类似overlap prefill）：当前该特性仅用于prefill，decode也可启用。预期减少5-15% decode延迟。

Let me create a comprehensive analysis based on the PLAN.md timing data and the code structure I've examined. ## Report: EAGLE-3 Draft Model Forward Time Breakdown Based on my analysis of the codebase and profiling data in `/user_4813494d/openbmb/PLAN.md`, here is the complete forward time breakdown for the EAGLE-3 draft model (M=1 decode, Marlin W4A16): ### **Draft Forward Path Operations** The draft model forward pass in `forward_draft_extend()` and `draft_forward()` (eagle_worker.py) executes this sequence: 1. **fc layer** (embed fusion): 12288→4096 - **Kernel**: Marlin W4A16 - **Measured time**: 16.5 µs - **Speedup vs BF16**: 2.2x 2. **Attention block** (single decoder layer): - **q_proj** (8192→4096): Marlin 14.4 µs (1.8x) - **k_proj** (8192→256): Marlin 59.7 µs (MARLIN IS SLOWER, use BF16 7.3 µs) - **v_proj** (8192→256): Marlin 59.7 µs (MARLIN IS SLOWER, use BF16 7.2 µs) - **o_proj** (4096→4096): Marlin 10.3 µs (1.8x) - **Subtotal (optimal)**: 14.4 + 7.3 + 7.2 + 10.3 = **39.2 µs** 3. **MLP (SwiGLU gates + up/down projection)**: - **gate_proj** (4096→16384): Marlin **16.5 µs** (6.3x faster than BF16 104.5 µs) ⚡ - **up_proj** (4096→16384): Marlin **16.5 µs** (6.3x faster than BF16 104.5 µs) ⚡ - **down_proj** (16384→4096): Marlin **18.7 µs** (5.8x faster than BF16 108.5 µs) ⚡ - **MLP Subtotal**: 16.5 + 16.5 + 18.7 = **51.7 µs** 4. **lm_head** (logits projection): 4096→32000 - **Note**: BF16 (not quantized, no benefit from FP4) - **Measured time**: 28.7 µs (6.9x vs BF16 198.9 µs, but operates on FP4→BF16 boundary) ### **Total Single-Step Forward Time (M=1)** **Hybrid optimal (using BF16 for k/v_proj, Marlin elsewhere)**: - fc: 16.5 - q_proj: 14.4 - k_proj (BF16): 7.3 - v_proj (BF16): 7.2 - o_proj: 10.3 - MLP: 51.7 - lm_head: 28.7 - **Total: ~136 µs (from PLAN.md Phase 2.2)** Compare to pure BF16: ~612 µs → **4.5x speedup with Marlin hybrid** ### **MLP Fraction of Total Forward Time** **MLP timing**: 51.7 µs **Total forward**: 136 µs **MLP fraction**: **51.7 / 136 = 38%** The MLP represents **just over one-third** of the total draft forward time, making it the single largest compute block after lm_head (28.7 µs / 21%). ### **Key Findings** 1. **gate_proj + up_proj dominate MLP cost** at 16.5 µs each (both 6.3x speedup from FP4) 2. **down_proj** is slightly slower (18.7 µs) due to larger output shape (16384→4096) 3. **Total MLP = 51.7 µs** is achievable only with FP4 quantization (W4A16 Marlin); BF16 would be ~317 […]

GLA State Tensors and Shapes **State Definition (MiniCPMLightningMixer, line 419 in minicpm.py):** ``` state_shape = (num_kv_heads, head_dim, head_dim) ``` For MiniCPM-SALA with 24 Lightning layers: - `num_kv_heads`: 16 / tp_size (typically 16 on single GPU) - `head_dim`: 128 - **Per-layer state shape**: (16, 128, 128) = 262,144 elements **Storage in MambaPool.SpeculativeState (memory_pool.py, lines 150-152):** ```python @dataclass(frozen=True, kw_only=True) class SpeculativeState(State): intermediate_ssm: torch.Tensor # [num_layers, batch_size+1, draft_tokens, H, D, D] intermediate_conv_window: List[torch.Tensor] # GLA only uses temporal, not conv ``` **Intermediate State Buffer Shape (memory_pool.py, lines 218-229):** ``` intermediate_ssm: [24, spec_state_size+1, speculative_num_draft_tokens, 16, 128, 128] ``` Where: - `24`: All 24 Lightning/GLA layers - `spec_state_size+1`: Max batch size + 1 (for padding) - `speculative_num_draft_tokens`: Default 5-7 draft tokens per sequence - `16, 128, 128`: The state tensor dimensions --- ### 2. How verify() Handles Tree Structure **Current Implementation (eagle_worker.py, lines 754-854):** ```python def verify(self, batch: ScheduleBatch, spec_info: EagleVerifyInput): spec_info.prepare_for_verify(batch, self.page_size) # Flattens tree batch.input_ids = self.draft_token # All tokens as flat sequence batch.forward_mode = ForwardMode.TARGET_VERIFY # Forward: processes ALL draft tokens in sequence batch_result = self.target_worker.forward_batch_generation( model_worker_batch, is_verify=True ) ``` **Tree Arrangement:** - **Draft tokens are arranged LINEARLY, not as a tree structure** - All `batch_size * draft_token_num` tokens concatenated - Example with topk=4 and 3 draft tokens per sequence: ``` Seq0_tok0 Seq0_tok1 Seq0_tok2 Seq1_tok0 Seq1_tok1 Seq1_tok2 ... ``` NOT: ``` Seq0_tok0 ├─ Seq0_tok1_option0 ├─ Seq0_tok1_option1 └─ Seq0_tok1_option2 ``` **Attention Mask Usage:** - For standard attention (MiniCPMAttention): Uses RadixAttention which supports tree masks - For GLA/Lightning (SimpleGLAAttnBackend): **NO tree mask handling** - Line 1616: `seq_len = forward_batch.spec_info.draft_token_num` - Does NOT reshape or apply per-sequence masks - Processes as flat sequence --- ### 3. GLA State Management During TARGET_VERIFY **Step-by-step State Processing (hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py, lines 1636-1711):** ```python if forward_batch.forward_mode.is_target_verify(): draft_token_num = forward_batch.spec_info.draft_token_num batch_size = forward_batch.batch_size # Reshape flat inputs: (1, bs*draft_token_num, H, D) → (1, bs, draft_token_num, H, D) q_steps = q.view(1, batch_size, draft_token_num, num_heads, head_dim) k_steps = k.view(1, batch_size, draft_token_num, num_heads, head_dim) v_steps = v.view(1, batch_size, draft_token_num, num_heads, head_dim) # Load initial state from permanent cache initial_state = layer_cache.temporal[mamba_indices, :] # (batch_size, H, D, D) current_state = initial_state for step in range(draft_token_num): # Extract single token per sequence sq = q_steps[:, :, step, :, :] # (1, batch_size, H, D) sk = k_steps[:, :, step, :, […]

完美！现在我有足够的信息来进行深入分析。让我生成完整的分析报告： ## 深度分析报告：SGLang EAGLE 推测解码 `draft_extend_after_decode` 的 960us 开销来源 基于对代码的彻底审查，我已识别出关键的开销点。总耗时 1.2ms 中，模型前向 241us，剩余约 960us 的开销源如下： --- ### 1. **forward_draft_extend_after_decode 方法** **文件：**/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker.py:1027-1115 #### 关键开销点： **L1030-1031：Tensor Clone 操作（约 50-100us）** ```python seq_lens_backup = batch.seq_lens.clone() seq_lens_cpu_backup = batch.seq_lens_cpu.clone() ``` - 对于批大小为 b 的请求，这是 GPU 上的张量 clone，会导致 GPU 内存复制和 CPU-GPU 同步 **L1038-1053：Conditional Batch Copy + EagleDraftInput 创建（约 30-50us）** ```python if not input_is_idle and batch.spec_info.verified_id.numel() == 0: batch = batch.copy() # ScheduleBatch.copy() - 浅拷贝，但包含引用更新 batch.prepare_for_idle() batch.spec_info = EagleDraftInput.create_idle_input(...) ``` - `batch.copy()` 虽是浅拷贝，但涉及对象构造 **L1057-1060：prepare_extend_after_decode（约 100-200us）** ```python batch.spec_info.prepare_extend_after_decode(batch, self.speculative_num_steps) ``` （见下文详细分析） **L1068：get_model_worker_batch（约 50-100us）** ```python model_worker_batch = batch.get_model_worker_batch() ``` （见下文详细分析） **L1070-1072：ForwardBatch.init_new（约 300-400us）** ```python forward_batch = ForwardBatch.init_new(model_worker_batch, self.draft_model_runner) ``` （见下文详细分析） **L1074, L1076：seq_lens_sum 计算 - GPU 同步点（约 20-30us）** ```python forward_batch.seq_lens_sum = forward_batch.seq_lens_cpu.sum().item() # CPU tensor sum + sync # or forward_batch.seq_lens_sum = batch.seq_lens.sum().item() # GPU tensor sum + GPU->CPU sync ``` - `.item()` 引发隐式 GPU 同步，即使是 CPU tensor 的 sum 操作也可能涉及 tensor 的创建开销 --- ### 2. **EagleDraftInput.prepare_extend_after_decode 方法** **文件：**/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_info.py:697-727 #### 关键开销点： **L707：Python 列表推导式（约 10-20us）** ```python batch.extend_lens = [x + 1 for x in batch.spec_info.accept_length_cpu] ``` - `accept_length_cpu` 是 Python list（在 verify() 中通过 `.tolist()` 生成） - 列表推导式涉及 Python 循环和整数加法，虽然小但在热路径上 **L708：Python sum() 函数（约 5-10us）** ```python batch.extend_num_tokens = sum(batch.extend_lens) ``` - 以下是完整的调查结果： --- ## CUDA Graph + Triton 内核行为：完整分析报告 ### 核心问题的直接回答 **Q1: CUDA graph 捕获什么？** CUDA graph 在 Python 层执行 `torch.cuda.graph()` 上下文时，**记录所有发出的 `cuLaunchKernel` 调用**，包括： - 编译好的 PTX binary 函数句柄（compile-time 决定） - Grid 维度（Python 计算后传入，**冻结在捕获时**） - Block 维度（同上） - 所有参数： - **Tensor 参数**: 记录 GPU 地址（指针），**不记录值**。Replay 时内核读到的是该地址的当前内容。 - **Scalar 参数**（如 B, T）: 记录**捕获时的具体数值**，replay 时永远使用该值。 实验验证（`/tmp/test_triton_cg2.py`）：对同一内核用 N=10 和 N=20 各建一个图，replay 后分别得到正确结果，互不干扰。 **Q2: `do_not_specialize` 与 CUDA graph 的关系？** `do_not_specialize=['B', 'T']` 的作用是**不将 B/T 的值烧录进 PTX binary**（否则每种 T 值需要编译一个 kernel）。但 CUDA graph 捕获的是 `cuLaunchKernel` 调用时的实参——scalar 值依然被冻结。 **对本场景无害**：每个 batch size 有独立的 CUDA graph，所以每个图捕获的 `T = bs * dtn` 与 replay 时一致。 **Q3: IS_VARLEN 下 `for step in range(0, T)` 的 T 是哪个？** 实验（`/tmp/test_loop_scalar.py`）直接证明：**IS_VARLEN 模式下，for 循环用的是本地 T（从 cu_seqlens 读取的 eos-bos），不是原始 arg T**。CUDA graph 下行为与 eager 完全一致，输出匹配。 **Q4: `all_tokens = B * T` 的 T 是原始 arg T（total_T），用于 `p_o` 偏移量计算。这在 CUDA graph 下是否安全？** 是安全的。原始 T arg 被冻结为 `bs * dtn`（捕获 bs 专用图），而 `p_o` 的偏移基于 `bos`（从 cu_seqlens 读取），两者均在同一图内保持一致。 --- ### `_fused_recurrent_gla_intermediate_kernel` 第 111 行的 `ht_all` 索引 ```python p_ht = ht_all + (i_nh * T + step) * K * V + ... ``` 此处 `T` 已被 IS_VARLEN 分支覆写为**本地 T = dtn**（per-seq 长度）。索引正确。 `ht_buf` 分配为 `(max_bs * H, dtn, K, V)` 而内核按 `i_nh * dtn + step` 线性访问，范围为 `[0, actual_bs*H*dtn*K*V - 1]`，在 bounds 内。 --- ### "有 CUDA graph 就出垃圾"的真正原因（按可能性排序） 经过所有实验，**Triton 内核本身没有 CUDA graph 兼容性问题**，问题在**调用层**： **最可能根因：`o_buf` 或 `ht_buf` 在 CUDA graph 捕获时未预分配** 代码路径（hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py 第 1794 行）： ```python if self._cg_fused_o_buf is not None: # CUDA graph path: 使用预分配 buffer ... else: # Eager path: 调用 _fused_recurrent_gla_with_intermediate # 此函数内部 q.new_empty(...) 动态分配！ ``` 若 `init_cuda_graph_state` 未在 capture 前调用，则 `_cg_fused_o_buf is None`，代码走 eager path，内部调用： ```python o_buf = q.new_empty(NK, *v.shape, dtype=torch.float32) # 图池分配！ ht_buf = q.new_empty(N * H, T_per_seq, K, V, dtype=torch.float32) # 图池分配！ ``` CUDA graph 的私有内存池**在 replay 间可以复用**。如果同一池被其他 op 使用，buffer 内容被破坏，产生垃圾。 **第二可能：cu_seqlens 使用了非预分配的临时张量** `_cg_verify_cu_seqlens` 是预分配的（从 index 0 开始的切片，GPU 地址即 base ptr）。但若在某条代码路径里用了 `torch.arange(..., device='cuda')` 新分配的 tensor，CUDA graph 捕获该 tensor 的 ptr，replay 时该 ptr 可能指向已回收或被覆写的内存。 **实验证明**（`/tmp/test_triton_corrupt.py`）：in-place 修改 cu_seqlens buffer，replay 后内核确实读到修改后的值——证明 pointer 被捕获而非 value。若 buffer 被意外覆写或池化回收，就产生垃圾。 **其他已排除**： - scalar T 冻结问题：不存在（per-bs 图保证一致） - IS_VARLEN 编译时常量：不存在（cu_seqlens 始终 non-None） - Grid 大小不一致：不存在（per-bs 图） - `for range(0, T)` 动态 T 问题：**不存在**，实验完全验证正确

至此我已经全面调查了所有相关代码路径。现在我来综合所有发现。 --- ## 调查总结 ### 1. accept_len 的统计逻辑（文件与行号） **文件**：`demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/managers/scheduler_metrics_mixin.py` - **更新**（第 115-118 行）：`update_spec_metrics(bs, num_accepted_tokens)` - `spec_num_accepted_tokens += num_accepted_tokens + bs`（`+bs` 是每个 req 必然接受的 confirmed token） - `spec_num_forward_ct += bs` - **计算**（第 341 行）：`accept_len = spec_num_accepted_tokens / spec_num_forward_ct` - **调用点**：`scheduler_output_processor_mixin.py` 第 380 行，每个 decode batch 结束后调用 - **数据来源**：`eagle_worker.py` 第 396 行，`num_accepted_tokens=sum(verify_output.accept_length_per_req_cpu)`，是所有 req 的额外接受 draft token 数之和 - 统计是**滚动窗口**（`decode_log_interval` 个 batch 内的累计），每次打印后重置 公式推导：`accept_len = 1 + sum(accept_length_per_req_cpu) / bs`。当所有 draft 都被拒绝时 accept_len = 1.0。 ### 2. 高并发 accept_len 骤降的原因（多层叠加） **原因一（最主要）：GLA sibling state 污染随 batch_size 非线性恶化** 文件：`hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py` 第 1241-1257 行（TARGET_VERIFY GLA forward），第 221-228 行注释（eagle3.md 已记录）。 MiniCPM-SALA 有 24 层 Lightning Attention（GLA）。TARGET_VERIFY 时，`topk=2` 树结构中两个分支（sibling）的 GLA state 相互污染，因为线性注意力无 tree mask，按线性序列处理所有 draft tokens。 - S1（bs=1）：1 个 req，污染只在 req 内部，影响有限 - S8（bs=8）：8 个 req，但每个 req 的 sibling 污染独立，8 个 req 的 GLA state 仍然是独立的 - **Smax（bs=64）：64 个请求并发时，draft 阶段 `num_seqs * topk = 64 * 2 = 128` 个 token，verify 阶段 `batch * dtn = 64 * 3 = 192` 个 token 全部线性展开，GLA 递归状态在 128/192 步内持续积累误差** 关键理解：**GLA 污染的累积量与 batch 内 token 数量有关，而非每个 req 独立**。高并发时整个 batch 的 GLA 状态形成一个长链，每个后续 req 的 draft 预测都受到前面 req 的污染状态影响。 **原因二（确认）：draft 阶段 spec_num_steps=1 时 CUDA graph runner 的 batch_size 覆盖** 文件：`eagle_draft_cuda_graph_runner.py` 第 65 行。spec_steps=1 时 `self.cuda_graph_runner = None`（第 311 行），完全不捕获 draft CUDA graph（只在 `speculative_num_steps > 1` 时才捕获）。高并发时 draft 走 eager 路径，每次调用都在计算图外运行，产生更多 Python overhead 并降低 GPU 利用率——但这只影响速度，不影响 accept_len。 **原因三（确认）：forward_batch_generation 路径的 num_accepted_tokens=0 回退** 文件：`eagle_worker.py` 第 356-370 行。当 `batch.forward_mode.is_extend() or batch.is_extend_in_batch` 时（prefill batch），返回 `num_accepted_tokens=0`。但非 DP attention 配置下 `is_extend_in_batch` 始终 False，所以高并发下 prefill batch 走 `process_batch_result_prefill`，不调用 `update_spec_metrics`，这条路径不是骤降原因。 **原因四（确认）：没有 BS_THRESHOLD 机制** 不像 Medusa（`medusa_worker.py` 第 340 行有 `if bs >= self.spec_bs_threshold: fallback`），EAGLE-3 没有任何 batch size 阈值来禁用 spec decode。所有 batch 都走 spec 路径，不存在"超过阈值 → fallback 导致 num_accepted_tokens=0"的情况。 **原因五（推断）：EAGLE-3 draft model 的 hidden_states 在高并发时质量下降** 文件：`model_runner.py` 第 580-582 行，EAGLE3 在 TARGET_VERIFY 时捕获 aux hidden states（layers [1, 10, 22] 的拼接，4096*3=12288 维）。`forward_draft_extend_after_decode` 用这些 hidden_states 为下一轮 draft 提供初始化。**当 batch_size 从 8 增到 64 时，draft model 的实际有效 batch 也从 8 增到 64，GLA state 的误差从每 req 独立计算变为所有 req 累积计算，导致 hidden_states 提取质量下降，从而降低 draft token 准确率。** ### 3. eval 阶段 accept_len=1.00 的特殊原因 eval 用 concurrency=32，所有 32 个请求几乎同时到达，会形成以下场景： 1. 大量 prefill 批次（32 个请求的初始化），每次 prefill 后 `update_spec_metrics` 不被调用 2. 第一个 decode batch 通常 `batch_size ≈ 32`，GLA sibling 污染极为严重（32 * 2 = 64 个 draft token 线性展开） 3. 更重要的是：eval 请求通常输出较长，但 **accept_len=1.00 发生在 eval 期间而非 bench 期间** eval 的 `accept_len=1.00` 最可能原因是：eval 评测集中的请求有特定的 stop token 规律（chain-of-thought + specific answer format），导致 draft model 在这种分布上几乎无法预测正确。eval 用 temperature=0，而 bench 也用 temperature=0，所以采样策略不是区分因素。 **更可能的根本原因**：eval 请求（80 题 MCQ + NIAH）是 OOD 分布，EAGLE-3 模型在这类结构化推理问题上 accept rate 本来就很低；高并发 bs=32 进一步放大了 GLA 污染。 ### 4. Spec-v2 的 topk>1 gate **位置**: `server_args.py` 第 2104-2110 行。 条件：当 `SGLANG_ENABLE_SPEC_V2=1` 且算法为 EAGLE/EAGLE3/STANDALONE 时，如果 `speculative_eagle_topk > 1`，直接 `raise ValueError("Spec v2 currently only supports topk = 1")`。这是一个 **hard gate**，服务器启动时就会报错退出。 这意味着当前 `eagle_worker_v2.py` 走的代码路径中 topk 只能为 1。 ### 2. minicpm_backend 标准 attention TARGET_VERIFY 不使用 tree mask 代码证据： - `MiniCPMSparseBackend`（第 150 行）继承 `AttentionBackend`，**未重写** `get_verify_buffers_to_fill_after_draft`（base 返回 `[None, None]`，第 60-66 行 `base_attn_backend.py`）。 - `eagle_worker_v2.py` 第 315-317 行调用 `get_verify_buffers_to_fill_after_draft()`，对 minicpm_backend 得到的 `tree_mask_buf = None, position_buf = None`。`build_tree_kernel_efficient` 接收 None buf，tree mask 被构建但 **不会写入任何 CUDA graph buffer**。 - TARGET_VERIFY 执行路径（第 972-1013 行）：FlashInfer `BatchPrefillWithPagedKVCacheWrapper` 调用 `forward()` 时传 `causal=True`，**没有任何 custom_mask 参数**。wrapper 的 `plan()` 也仅传 `causal=True`（第 1815-1828 行），无 mask 字段。 - 对比 triton_backend（第 777-783 行），它返回 `[self.cuda_graph_custom_mask, None]` 并在 verify 时使用 `generate_attn_arg_prefill` 返回的 `self.custom_mask`。minicpm_backend 完全绕过了这条路径。 **结论**：minicpm_backend 的标准 attention 在 TARGET_VERIFY 中做的是 **纯 causal prefill**（每个 request 的 dtn 个 token 作为 query，causal mask 自动保证 token 只看到自己之前的 KV），**不使用 tree mask**。 ### 3. 对当前配置的影响 当前使用 `spec_steps=1, topk=2`： - **spec_v2 无法使用**：topk=2 触发 hard gate（第 2108 行），必须走 spec_v1 路径（`eagle_worker.py`，非 `_v2`）。 - **spec_v1 路径**中，`EagleVerifyInput.generate_attn_arg_prefill` 确实构建了 `custom_mask`（第 158-214 行），但 minicpm_backend 的 TARGET_VERIFY 路径根本不调用 `generate_attn_arg_prefill`——它自行构建 FlashInfer prefill wrapper 并只用 `causal=True`。 - 因此即使在 spec_v1 中，minicpm_backend 的 8 个标准 attention 层的 tree verify **也是 flat causal**，不使用 tree mask。这对 topk=2 树结构意味着 sibling token 之间互相可见，存在注意力污染。不过实际仅有 3 个 token（1 verified + 2 draft），污染有限。

Here is the complete analysis: --- ## SimpleGLA TARGET_VERIFY 分析报告 ### 1. Fused Kernel 实现状态 **已实现自定义 fused kernel**。第 51-131 行定义了 `_fused_recurrent_gla_intermediate_kernel`，这是一个自定义 Triton 内核，在单次 kernel launch 中处理所有 draft_token_num 步骤，同时保存每步中间状态用于 rollback。注释明确说明目的是"Eliminates 3x kernel launch overhead in TARGET_VERIFY"（第 48 行）。 TARGET_VERIFY forward 路径（第 1807-1848 行）分两条路径： - **CUDA graph 路径**（第 1815-1836 行）：使用预分配 buffer `_cg_fused_o_buf`/`_cg_fused_ht_buf`/`_cg_fused_o_sum`，调用 `_fused_recurrent_gla_launch`，零动态分配 - **Eager 路径**（第 1837-1848 行）：调用 `_fused_recurrent_gla_with_intermediate` 自分配 buffer ### 2. BK/BV Tiling 与带宽开销 自定义 kernel 和 fla 库的 `fused_recurrent` 使用**完全相同的 tiling**： - `BK = min(next_power_of_2(K), 64) = 64`（K=128） - `BV = min(next_power_of_2(V), 64) = 64`（V=128） - `NK = cdiv(128, 64) = 2`, `NV = cdiv(128, 64) = 2` - Grid: `(NV=2, NK=2, N*H)` → 每个 head 4 个 block 每个 block 持有 `BK*BV = 64*64 = 4096` float32 的 state tile（16 KB SMEM），总 state 128*128*4B = 64 KB/head。`ht_buf` 大小为 `max_bs * H * draft_token_num * K * V * 4B`，对于 bs=64, H=16, dtn=3: 64*16*3*128*128*4 = 192 MB float32。 Decode 路径（第 1853 行）调用 fla 的 `fused_recurrent_simple_gla`，**同样是 BK=BV=64**（fla 第 352 行确认），并非 128。CLAUDE.md 中提到的"decode 专用 BK=BV=128"可能指之前的实验或其他 backend。 ### 3. Tree-Aware 缺失 `cu_seqlens` 是 flat 等间隔的（第 1672-1674 行）： ```python cu_seqlens = arange(0, bs*dtn+1, step=dtn) ``` 这意味着每个请求的 draft tokens 被视为**连续线性序列**，没有 tree mask。对于 topk=1（Medusa K=1），这是正确的。但对于 EAGLE-3 topk>1 的 tree verify： - GLA 的 recurrent state 会被**所有 sibling 节点顺序污染**（第 112-125 行的 step 循环无条件累积所有 token） - 被 reject 的 sibling 的 k*v 外积已经写入 state，无法撤销 - `intermediate_ssm` gather/scatter（第 1584-1586 行）按 `track_steps` 选择中间状态，但 state 本身已被污染 ### 4. HybridAttnBackend Verify Buffer Hooks HybridAttnBackend（`hybrid_attn_backend.py`）**未转发** `get_verify_buffers_to_fill_after_draft` 和 `update_verify_buffers_to_fill_after_draft`。搜索确认这两个方法名在该文件中完全不存在。它继承 `AttentionBackend` 基类的默认实现（第 60-66 行返回 `[None, None]`，第 68-77 行 raise `NotImplementedError`）。 这意味着 spec decode 框架无法通过这些 hooks 向 SimpleGLA 传递 tree mask 或 position 信息。 ### 关键发现总结 | 项目 | 状态 | |------|------| | Fused kernel | 已实现，BK=BV=64，含中间状态导出 | | CUDA graph 安全 | 已实现，所有 buffer 预分配 | | Tree-aware verify | 缺失，flat cu_seqlens，sibling 污染 | | Verify buffer hooks | HybridAttnBackend 未转发，基类默认返回 None | | Decode vs Verify BK/BV | 完全相同（均为 64），无差异 |

Here is the analysis: --- ## FlashInfer vs Triton DRAFT_EXTEND 性能差异分析 ### 核心差异：replay 阶段的 metadata 更新机制 **FlashInfer 路径**（每次 CUDA graph replay 时执行）： 1. `eagle_draft_extend_cuda_graph_runner.py:446` 调用 `init_forward_metadata_replay_cuda_graph` 2. `flashinfer_backend.py:713-724` 进入 DRAFT_EXTEND 分支，调用 `indices_updater_prefill.update` 3. `flashinfer_backend.py:1231` 调用 `call_begin_forward`（prefill updater 版本） 4. `flashinfer_backend.py:1382-1389` 先调用 `spec_info.generate_attn_arg_prefill`（eagle_info.py:158-214）： - 分配 `qo_indptr` 张量（`torch.arange`） - 分配 `cum_kv_seq_len` 张量 - 分配 `kv_indices` 张量（`torch.empty`） - 启动 `create_flashinfer_kv_indices_triton` kernel - 可能扩展 `custom_mask` 5. **关键开销** `flashinfer_backend.py:1427-1445`：调用 `wrapper_paged.begin_forward()`（即 FlashInfer 的 `plan()` 函数），传入 qo_indptr、kv_indptr、kv_indices 等参数。这是 FlashInfer 内部的 C++ 调度逻辑，负责计算 split-KV 配置、分配工作空间、准备 CUDA kernel 参数。 **Triton 路径**（每次 CUDA graph replay 时执行）： 1. 同样 `eagle_draft_extend_cuda_graph_runner.py:446` 调用 `init_forward_metadata_replay_cuda_graph` 2. `triton_backend.py:752-768` 进入 DRAFT_EXTEND 分支： - 就地更新 `qo_indptr`（`torch.cumsum`，就地写入预分配 buffer） - 就地更新 `kv_indptr`（`torch.cumsum`） - 启动 `create_flashinfer_kv_indices_triton` kernel - **没有任何 begin_forward/plan 调用**，直接结束 3. 后续 `forward_extend`（L838-858）直接读取 `self.forward_metadata` 中的 indptr/indices 张量调用 Triton kernel ### 三大性能差异来源 **1. `begin_forward()`/`plan()` 开销（最主要）** FlashInfer 的 `wrapper_paged.begin_forward()` 是 C++ 侧的 plan 函数，需要：解析 indptr 计算工作分配、确定 split-KV 策略、设置 workspace buffer 偏移。即使用了 `non_blocking=True`，这仍然是一个 **CPU 侧的同步调用**，涉及 Python→C++ 跨边界 + 内部状态更新。Triton 完全没有这个阶段——metadata 都是 GPU 张量，forward 时直接传给 Triton kernel。 **2. 动态张量分配 vs 就地更新** FlashInfer 的 `generate_attn_arg_prefill`（eagle_info.py:167-185）每次 replay 都 **新建** `qo_indptr`、`cum_kv_seq_len`、`kv_indices` 张量。Triton 在 `init_forward_metadata_capture_cuda_graph` 时预分配好 `self.qo_indptr`、`self.kv_indptr`、`self.cuda_graph_kv_indices`，replay 时只做 **就地 cumsum + kernel 填充**，零分配。 **3. 高并发线性放大** bs=64 时，上述开销（plan 调用 + 张量分配）与 bs 线性相关：kv_indices 长度 = sum(seq_lens)，indptr 长度 = bs+1，plan 内部遍历所有 bs 个请求计算 split 策略。Triton 的 cumsum 和 triton kernel 在 GPU 上并行执行，对 bs 不敏感。 ### 结论 FlashInfer 在 DRAFT_EXTEND CUDA graph replay 时的瓶颈是 **每次 replay 都要调用 `begin_forward()`/`plan()` + 分配临时张量**。Triton 通过预分配 buffer + 就地更新 + 无 plan 阶段，完全避免了这些 CPU 侧开销。对于 draft model 这种 1 层 decoder、极轻量计算的场景，这个 metadata 准备的固定开销占总时间比例很高，因此 Triton 显著更快。

Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile the findings. Here is the comprehensive report on Triton `tl.dot` precision behavior on SM120/Blackwell: --- ## 1. `tl.dot` Function Signature and Defaults The official function signature from [triton/python/triton/language/core.py](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/blob/main/python/triton/language/core.py): ```python def dot(input, other, acc=None, input_precision=None, allow_tf32=None, max_num_imprecise_acc=None, out_dtype=float32, ...) ``` Key defaults: - **`out_dtype`**: defaults to `tl.float32` -- the output/accumulator is always FP32 unless explicitly overridden - **`input_precision`**: defaults to `"tf32"` for NVIDIA GPUs, but this parameter **only applies when inputs are f32** (float32). When inputs are bf16 or fp16, `input_precision` is **ignored** ## 2. Behavior When `out_dtype` Is NOT Specified vs When It IS `tl.float32` **They are identical.** The default `out_dtype` is already `tl.float32`. Whether you write `tl.dot(a, b)` or `tl.dot(a, b, out_dtype=tl.float32)`, the behavior is the same -- the accumulator and output are FP32. For bf16 inputs, the MMA instruction generated is: `mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f32.bf16.bf16.f32` -- bf16 inputs, f32 accumulator. This is confirmed on SM120 and all prior architectures with tensor cores. ## 3. `input_precision` Parameter on SM120/Blackwell The `input_precision` parameter has these options (for NVIDIA): `"tf32"`, `"tf32x3"`, `"ieee"`, `"bf16x3"`, `"bf16x6"`. **All of these only apply when both input operands are f32.** For bf16/fp16 inputs, this parameter has no effect whatsoever -- the tensor core uses native bf16/fp16 MMA regardless of this setting. On SM120 specifically: - TF32 truncation applies to f32 inputs (truncates mantissa to 10 bits without rounding, which can bias results -- documented behavior) - For bf16 inputs, the precision path is identical to SM80/SM89/SM90 ## 4. Known SM120 Issues with Triton Several confirmed issues found: ### Issue [#7550](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/issues/7550) -- `tl.dot_scaled` Falls Back to BF16 MMA On RTX 5090 (SM120), `tl.dot_scaled` (the scaled dot product for MXFP4/MXFP8) was always compiling down to `mma.sync.aligned.m16n8k16.row.col.f32.bf16.bf16.f32` instead of native FP4/FP8 MMA instructions. This was fixed by [PR #7918](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/pull/7918) which added native MXFP FP8 MMA for SM120, though it was reverted and reapplied due to regressions. ### Issue [#7188](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/issues/7188) -- float8 `tl.dot` Runs with FP16 MMA on 5090 FP8 dot products were incorrectly running with fp16 MMA instructions on SM120 instead of native FP8 MMA. ### Issue [#9830](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/issues/9830) -- Incorrect `tl.dot` Results FP16 matmul producing ~4.5% mismatched elements vs PyTorch reference (on RTX 3090, not SM120 specifically, but indicates general precision fragility). ### Issue [#4574](https://github.com/triton-lang/triton/issues/4574) -- TF32 Less Accurate Than FP16 TF32 matrix multiplication shown to be significantly less accurate than FP16 in relative error, due to mantissa truncation without rounding. ### […]

Here is the comprehensive report of findings from thorough web research on SM120/Blackwell/RTX 6000D kernel optimization strategies for LLM inference. --- ## 1. Marlin FP4 Kernel Tile Tuning **Current state**: Marlin FP4 GEMV is a fixed-configuration kernel. The tile size is hardcoded at 16x16 (`tile_size = 16` in `marlin_utils.py`). There are **no user-configurable tile size parameters** for Marlin FP4. **SM120-specific findings**: - On SM120, Marlin W4A16 fallback **outperforms** native CUTLASS NVFP4 for decode (M<=48). Benchmarks show 201 tok/s (Marlin) vs 160 tok/s (Triton MXFP4) on RTX PRO 6000 -- the vLLM PR #31089 attempting a Triton MXFP4 backend for SM120 was **closed** because it was 20% slower than Marlin. - The sgl-kernel Marlin FP4 GEMV uses CUTLASS 3.x collective builders internally with mma_tiler_mnk = (128, 1, 64) for the GEMV path. - **No tunable knobs exist** beyond the `SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD` you already use. The kernel template is specialized at compile time. - **Actionable**: Your current threshold=48 is already well-chosen. No further Marlin tile tuning is possible without modifying the CUDA kernel source. **Key insight from the NVFP4 GEMV blog post (Twelve Attempts)**: The top solutions in kernel competitions used: - PTX instructions (`cvt.rn.f16x2.e2m1x2`) instead of C intrinsics - Cache policy discrimination: matrix A with `L1::no_allocate`, vector B with `L1::evict_last` - 128-256 bit vectorized loads (`ld.global.v2.u64`, `ld.global.v4.u64`) - Aggressive register budget (`-maxrregcount=32` vs typical 80) - The kernel is **fundamentally memory-bound**, not compute-bound Sources: - [NVFP4 GEMV Blog](https://veitner.bearblog.dev/nvfp4-gemv/) - [Twelve Attempts at FP4 Kernel](https://amandeepsp.github.io/blog/nvfp4-blackwell-gemv/) - [vLLM MXFP4 Triton SM120 PR (closed)](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/31089) --- ## 2. CUTLASS FP4 Tile Configs for SM120 **Current tile configuration**: The only valid CUTLASS configuration for SM120 NVFP4 is: - ThreadBlockShape: `Shape<_128, _128, _128>` - ClusterShape: `Shape<_1, _1, _1>` (SM120 does **not** support multicast TMA, so cluster must be 1x1x1) - KernelSchedule: `KernelScheduleAuto` (defaults to cooperative; also supports pingpong) - Alignment: 32 elements for A/B, 128-bit for C/D - StageCount: `StageCountAutoCarveout` (auto-determined) **Critical SM120 constraints**: - Shared memory: **128 KB per SM**, but max per thread block is **99 KB** (vs B200's ~228 KB) - Max concurrent warps per SM: 48 - Max thread blocks per SM: 32 - No multicast TMA support (unlike SM100/B200) - Requires `compute_120f` suffix for full feature set (CUDA 13.0+) **cuDNN vs CUTLASS**: cuDNN **outperforms CUTLASS in all FP4 scenarios** on SM120, even with StreamK. This is documented in SGLang issue […]

Here is the comprehensive SM120/Blackwell kernel-level optimization report for your competition setup. --- ## SM120 (RTX 6000D) 硬件规格确认 - **GPU**: NVIDIA RTX 6000D, compute capability 12.0 (SM120) - **SM 数量**: 156 - **VRAM**: 84 GB GDDR7 - **显存带宽**: ~1.6-1.8 TB/s - **SM 共享内存**: 128 KB/SM (max 99 KB/block), 对比 B200 的 228 KB/SM - **最大线程束/SM**: 48 (对比 SM100 的 64) - **MMA 指令**: mma.sync (不是 tcgen05), 支持 FP4 m16n8k64 - **CUDA**: 12.9, cuDNN: 9.20 --- ## 优化发现 (按优先级排序) ### 1. [高优先级] 升级 FlashInfer CUTLASS FP4 GEMM 后端 — cuDNN 可能更快 **现状**: 你的代码中 `enable_flashinfer_fp4_gemm=True`, 使用 `flashinfer.mm_fp4` 默认 `cutlass` 后端。 **发现**: SGLang SM120 性能优化计划明确指出: "cuDNN outperforms CUTLASS implementation in all scenarios for NVFP4 x NVFP4, even though CUTLASS has StreamK"。你已有 cuDNN 9.20, 满足 cuDNN FP4 GEMM 的最低要求 (9.15+)。 **操作**: 设置环境变量 `SGLANG_FLASHINFER_FP4_GEMM_BACKEND=cudnn` 或命令行 `--fp4-gemm-backend flashinfer_cudnn`。 **预期加速**: CUTLASS 路径上的所有 GEMM 都可能加速, 特别是 prefill 阶段。可能有 5-15% 的端到端改善, 需要实测。 **难度**: 极低 — 只是配置变更。但需验证与你的 hybrid Marlin 逻辑兼容。 **风险**: cuDNN FP4 在 SM120 上可能有 `cudnnGraphNotSupportedError` (issue #2577)。FlashInfer 0.6.7.post3 的版本可能已修复, 但需实测。 **源**: [SGLang issue #19637](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/issues/19637), [FlashInfer issue #2577](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/issues/2577) --- ### 2. [高优先级] FlashInfer SM120 FMHA_V2 注意力内核被阻断 **现状**: 你的 FlashInfer cubin 包中有 7634 个 SM100a FMHA cubin, **零个 SM120 cubin**。`determine_attention_backend()` 对 SM120 直接 fallback 到 `"fa2"` (Ampere 级别), 因为 SM120 既不是 SM90 (FA3) 也不是 SM100。 **发现**: FlashInfer issue #2555 明确指出: SM120 FMHA_V2 内核已存在代码中, 但被三个 wiring 问题阻断: 1. `ENABLE_SM120` 环境变量未设置, SM120 cubin 从未生成 2. 后端选择器硬编码只检查 SM90, SM120 落入通用 FA2 3. XQA MLA 路径也未为 SM120 启用 **操作**: 升级 FlashInfer 到包含 SM120 FMHA_V2 修复的版本 (0.6.8+), 或设置 `ENABLE_SM120=1` 从源码重新构建。但这对 MiniCPM-SALA 的混合注意力只影响 8 个标准 attention 层。 **预期加速**: 对 8 个标准 Attention 层的 decode 可能有 20-40% 提升 (FA2→FMHA_V2 是代际跳跃)。但因为只有 8/32 层用标准注意力, 端到端影响可能 5-10%。 **难度**: 中高 — 需要升级 FlashInfer 或从源码编译, 且需确保兼容 `minicpm_flashinfer` 自定义后端。 **源**: [FlashInfer issue #2555](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/issues/2555), [FlashInfer issue #2649](https://github.com/flashinfer-ai/flashinfer/issues/2649) --- ### 3. [中优先级] Marlin FP4 — 当前已是 SM120 最优 decode 方案 **现状**: 你的 `common_ops.abi3.so` 编译于 `sm_120a`, Marlin decode threshold=48。 **发现**: 多方证据表明 Marlin W4A16 在 SM120 的小 M decode 场景中仍然是最优选择: - vLLM 社区报告: Marlin 在 SM120 上达到 42-50 tok/s (vs CUTLASS NVFP4 的 14-39 tok/s) - Marlin 使用的 mma.sync 指令在 SM120 上是原生支持的 - sgl-kernel 的 gptq_marlin_gemm 已编译为 SM120 native **关键限制**: Marlin 的 CUDA 源码仅针对 SM80 tile 配置, 没有 SM120 特定的 tile tuning。SM120 有更大的 L2 (192 MB vs 50 MB) 和不同的 SM 数量 (156), tile 配置可能不是最优。 **可能操作**: 如果能从源码重新编译 sgl-kernel, 调整 Marlin 的 `thread_m_blocks`, `thread_n_blocks`, `thread_k_blocks` 参数可能有收益, 但投入产出比不确定。 **难度**: 高 — 需要修改 CUDA kernel 源码并重新编译。 **源**: [vLLM […]
